# M7-R0a numerical floors

Companion to [m7-r0a.md](m7-r0a.md). No gain fit or pixel rejection. E/C mean Epic/Cinematic; SW means software mesh-SDF; SC means hardware surface-cache lighting. SSR rows are informative only. Renderlab has environment-only reflections.

Errors R and L are RGB relative RMS and luminance relative L1 (%); ΔE is signed energy bias (%). D/P are display RMS/p99 maximum-channel absolute error in 8-bit F0 codes at the stated exposure. N is the reference split-half RMS (%), not a candidate error allowance. Every JSON population also records split display noise at both exposures.

## Roughness, off-screen and feature populations

Exact roughness rows are retained so future tier cutoffs do not require a rerender. The convenience bands are B0 [0,.05), B1 [.05,.4), B2 [.4,1]. Reflected-feature populations are ideal centre-reflection guides; the final image is scored unchanged.

## Reflected feature position, energy and blur

E/Eref is the full RGB energy inside the reference footprint. C/Cref is target-colour contrast energy over the entire reflector. Position is the colour centroid's Euclidean error in pixels. Blur is candidate minus reference 10–90% edge width; missing edges remain misses. A dash means the definition is inapplicable or the candidate feature is missing, never zero.

## Adjacent-frame residuals and visible trails

Residuals are frame-to-frame changes of candidate-minus-reference display error. Trails require excess target-colour contrast above two display codes in an old-only reflection footprint. Their extent is Chebyshev distance from the current footprint; >32 is a lower bound. These values do not replace the complete-image or missing-feature errors.

## Motion-integrated reference convergence

Centred 180-degree shutter, 8/16 midpoint nodes, 256 samples per node. The 8-minus-16 difference includes Monte Carlo noise. N16 is actual-half display noise, preserving shared-seed covariance between time nodes. These do not score zero-shutter native images.

| Fixture / frame | 8 vs 16 D −14 | N16 −14 | 8 vs 16 D −10 | N16 −10 | Centre vs 16 D −14 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RF3 / 48 | 0.195 | 1.159 | 0.153 | 2.213 | 2.149 |
| RF3 / 64 | 0.195 | 1.260 | 0.117 | 2.348 | 2.866 |
| RF5 / 48 | 0.031 | 1.182 | 0.051 | 1.773 | 1.348 |
| RF5 / 64 | 0.028 | 1.217 | 0.044 | 1.734 | 1.566 |

## Functional telemetry, not a performance claim

CSV median/p95 over 64 frames with async at its default; separate single instrumented ProfileGPU frame with async off. Foreign compilers were active. ProfileGPU parents overlap; do not sum them. Memory is the CSV transient resource allocation and ray-tracing geometry, not total process/VRAM usage. All Lumen component allocations remain in native-telemetry.json.
